- [ ] **ADAPT-1 · Cursor and Antigravity adapters from local sessions** · P3 · `data` `native` — Yash, 29 October: after P2 is complete and the VIZ-1 ideas are started, and only if time remains before 18 November, integrate Cursor and Antigravity from the sessions that exist on this machine and test them; Claude Code and Codex remain the main harnesses. Counted on 29 Oct (file and folder counts only, nothing opened): **Cursor:** `~/.cursor` 159 files; `workspaceStorage` 9 workspace folders and 10 `state.vscdb` files. **Antigravity:** `~/.gemini/antigravity` 61 `conversations` and 61 `brain` folders, 28 `annotations`, 1 `browser_recordings`, 3013 files in all; `~/.antigravity` 4,803 files. Formats not yet examined.
  - **Antigravity format, Opus, 12 Nov (read on this machine; names, types and counts only):** sessions live in `~/.gemini/antigravity/conversations/`: 42 per-conversation SQLite files (`<id>.db`, WAL mode) and 21 older `<id>.pb` files. The `.pb` files do not compress at all (36,220,749 bytes → 36,226,332 gzipped), so they are encrypted or compressed and stay unsupported unless that changes. The `.db` files are readable: tables `trajectory_meta`, `steps` (idx, step_type, status, has_subtrajectory, and protobuf blobs metadata, error_details, permissions, task_details, render_info, step_payload, step_format), `gen_metadata`, `executor_metadata`, `parent_references`, `trajectory_metadata_blob`. The blobs are plain protobuf (they start with a field-1 tag and compress to 45%), but Google publishes no schema, so step types (one sampled session: 132 ×192, 15 ×62, 101 ×3, 14, 23) and fields must be decoded from evidence, and unknown ones stay `unknown`. Other local sources: `brain/<id>/` (plans, task lists and walkthrough artifacts, plus uploads), `browser_recordings/`, `conversation_summaries.db`. Unlike Claude Code and Codex, the source is a live SQLite file, not an append-only JSONL, so the adapter needs a read-only, WAL-aware polling source by `steps.idx`. No controller support. Estimate: a half-day decoding spike first, then about 2 to 4 agent-days for the adapter, fixtures and acceptance.
  - **Superseded for Antigravity (13 Nov, Yash requested it as P0):** the half-day spike and the polling plan above are replaced by [the research](review/antigravity-harness.md) and AG-1 to AG-6. Key correction: each conversation also has an append-only JSONL transcript (`brain/<id>/.system_generated/logs/transcript_full.jsonl`), which is the primary source; the SQLite file is metadata and enrichment, and two thirds of its steps are `CLEARED` (results dropped). ADAPT-1 now covers Cursor only.
  - **Do:** (1) a short research note first (what each product records, in what format, whether tool calls, file edits and outputs are present, whether it is encrypted or unstable between versions), read-only and with counts and shapes only; (2) an honest decision per product: supported, partly supported, or not supported because the data is not there; (3) for a supported one, a read-only adapter following [CODEBASE: how to add a harness](CODEBASE.md), redaction before persisting, with any event-schema change agreed by both builders first; (4) a `check:real` case.
  - **Accept:** real local sessions of each supported product import with counts matching the source, render in the existing history, Files and Git review at 600 and 1440 px, and Claude Code and Codex acceptance does not regress. If a product cannot be read honestly, the item records why and stops.

- [ ] **TEST-2 · Rotating flaky browser specs** · P1 · `rel` — **Completed 5 November in PR #331 (awaiting other-builder review):** three consecutive complete commands on 6009c09 each pass 654 tests / 28 existing skips / zero failures/retries at DRISHYA_WORKERS=3 plus serial=1, with a normal UI build each time (810.10 / 765.74 / 745.42 s). [Completion evidence](review/test-2-live-anchor.md). **Historical investigations:** **2 November, what is left (taken over from #314 by Yash with Claude, unclaimed again):** `b2-live.spec.ts:63`, the 5 px anchor offset, which also fails on clean main, cause not verified. Everything else #314 found is fixed and merged; [report](review/test-2-live-anchor.md). **20 Oct, main cause found and fixed:** a prompt-rail chapter jump never turned follow off (keyboard navigation does), so the next projection update ran `followLatest` and pulled the view back to the bottom about 150 ms after the jump. It only showed in a warm browser, which is why `turn-fold` and `prompt-timeline` failed whenever another spec file had run first in the same worker, and passed alone. The rail now sets `follow: false`, as the "deliberate navigation stops following" rule requires (the "Back to latest" pill returns to the edge). After the fix the five-file batch (fix-chain, fresh-workspace, hover-fit, product-workspace, settings-typography) passed 117/117 twice, and the full suite on main + DATA-1/2 gave 286 passed, 1 failed. **21 Oct, fixed:** `read-before-write` failed in a full run because a pointer click on Expand moved the pointer over the card first, which can start hover reading and move the card under the click; the three specs that expand a card (`read-before-write`, `fix-chain`, `plan-checklist`) now press Expand with the keyboard and check `aria-expanded`, by the button's class since its name changes to Close (9/9 repeated). `fix-chain` expands the failed run again, as its title says. `review-questions` failed the same way (timed out on the inspector after clicking the card's ⓘ): after a pointer click expands a card, the pointer rests on its body, hover reading engages after its dwell and moves the card, so the next click misses. It now uses the keyboard too (3/3). Specs that click inside a card after expanding it should do the same. **Still open (21 Oct):** `collection-surfaces.spec.ts:48` fails on main too (focus not on the expected row); `product-workspace.spec.ts:84` (studio long changes, hover focus) and `connected-workspace.spec.ts:533` fail intermittently on main and branches. `settings-typography` failed in two full runs: modern controls transition their colour over 120 ms, and in a warm browser (any spec file run first) the spec measured the close button's ✕ mid-transition after switching palette (3.88–4.43 against 4.5). Reproduced every time with one file first; the spec now waits for running animations before measuring, and passes in that order. Also fixed: `connected-workspace.spec.ts:79` (modern) timed out on main because modern Home also lists sessions discovered on the machine and, since UI-10, pages long lists; the fixture (dated 7 Oct) fell past the first page on a machine with newer real sessions. The spec now finds it through Home's Find sessions box (18/18 pass). **Still open:** `hover-fit.spec.ts:51` (codex), **fixed 21 Oct:** the codex session mounts exactly 5 cards and the spec needs more than 4 focused, so one hover that missed its 2 s window under load failed it (line 70, `found.length > 4`). Each card now gets one retry; a card hover reading really declines declines again, so the bound is unchanged (6/6 repeated runs). Previously: it failed only in the full suite; it passes 8/8 alone and after another file, so its cause is not found yet. Original report: found 20 Oct while gating PRs #170–#173: a different handful of specs fails on each full run, and the same ones fail on plain `origin/main`, so no PR is the cause. Seen: `hover-fit.spec.ts:51` (codex), `settings-typography.spec.ts:19`, `turn-fold.spec.ts:61` (header 39442 vs 39443 px), `prompt-timeline.spec.ts:22` (833 px where ≤ 1 expected), `product-workspace.spec.ts:84` and `:777` (`toBeInViewport`, `toHaveAttribute`), and "Workspace large draft comparison" in `fresh-workspace.spec.ts`. Most pass alone and fail under the parallel full run. **Do:** reproduce each alone, with `--workers=1` and in the full run; tell test timing (waiting on a layout or paged load) from real app instability (sub-pixel layout, rail position, viewport state); fix the spec or the app; record causes here. **Accept:** three consecutive full runs with zero failures, or each remaining failure recorded with its cause. This gates every merge, so it comes before more feature work. **22 Oct (PR #196, spec-only):** four more causes fixed. (1) Pointer rests on an expanded card: `workspace-navigation:10` and `ui-recovery:17` failed when hover reading moved the card under the ⓘ click; the new `expandCard` helper in `history-gestures.ts` expands with the keyboard, and actions inside the card use `press('Enter')`. Also applied to the latent sites in `p1-pacing`, `collection-surfaces` and `typed-items`. (2) `workspace-toolbar:88` (line 115, received 0): the trace showed the wheel gestures landed before the demo's history had content, so the up-wheel did nothing and the down-wheel had nothing to scroll; the spec now waits until history can scroll. Not an app bug. (3) `captured-resources:11`: `waitForLoadState` on a `noopener` popup never got its load event under load (the routed response was served in 13 ms); it now polls the popup URL and the route hit. (4) `fresh-workspace:1766` (12 px icon/label offset): `openComposer` collected animations in the click's frame, before the opening transition started; `settled()` waits two frames first. **Overload-only, left unchanged:** under an artificial 26-thread CPU burner, `fresh-workspace:1552`, `git-review:146` and `repository-navigation:25` missed their 10 s waits (slow Home/Git/repository scans); none has failed at normal load. `collection-surfaces:48`, `prompt-timeline:22`, `fresh-workspace:1048` and `workspace-toolbar` did not reproduce alone, in their warm prefix group (3×) or under load. **Accepted 22 Oct:** after #196, three consecutive full runs on the reference machine each gave 295 passed, 26 skipped, 0 failed (15.7–16.2 min).
